from collections import deque

def bfs(graph, start):
    visited = set()
    queue = deque([start])
    visited.add(start)
    order = []

    while queue:
        vertex = queue.popleft()
        order.append(vertex)
        for neighbor in graph[vertex]:
            if neighbor not in visited:
                visited.add(neighbor)
                queue.append(neighbor)
    return order


============================================================

def find_min(arr):
    if not arr:
        return None
    result = arr[0]
    for x in arr[1:]:
        if x < result:
            result = x
    return result


============================================================

def factorial(n):
    result = 1
    for i in range(2, n + 1):
        result *= i
    return result


============================================================

def count_vowels(s):
    count = 0
    for ch in s.lower():
        if ch in 'aeiou':
            count += 1
    return count

def lcm(a, b):
    return abs(a * b) // gcd(a, b) if a and b else 0


============================================================

def gcd(a, b):
    while b:
        a, b = b, a % b
    return a


============================================================

def reverse_string(s):
    return s[::-1]

def find_max(arr):
    if not arr:
        return None
    result = arr[0]
    for x in arr[1:]:
        if x > result:
            result = x
    return result


============================================================

def knapsack(weights, values, capacity):
    n = len(weights)
    dp = [[0 for _ in range(capacity + 1)] for _ in range(n + 1)]

    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        for w in range(capacity + 1):
            dp[i][w] = dp[i-1][w]
            if weights[i-1] <= w:
                val = dp[i-1][w - weights[i-1]] + values[i-1]
                if val > dp[i][w]:
                    dp[i][w] = val
    return dp[n][capacity]


============================================================

def merge_sort(arr):
    if len(arr) <= 1:
        return arr
    mid = len(arr) // 2
    left = merge_sort(arr[:mid])
    right = merge_sort(arr[mid:])
    return merge(left, right)

def merge(left, right):
    result = []
    i = j = 0
    while i < len(left) and j < len(right):
        if left[i] <= right[j]:
            result.append(left[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            result.append(right[j])
            j += 1
    result.extend(left[i:])
    result.extend(right[j:])
    return result


============================================================

def sum_list(arr):
    if not arr:
        return 0
    return sum(arr)


============================================================

class Node:
    def __init__(self, data):
        self.data = data
        self.next = None

class LinkedList:
    def __init__(self):
        self.head = None

    def append(self, data):
        new_node = Node(data)
        if not self.head:
            self.head = new_node
            return
        current = self.head
        while current.next:
            current = current.next
        current.next = new_node

    def find(self, data):
        current = self.head
        while current:
            if current.data == data:
                return True
            current = current.next
        return False


============================================================

def remove_duplicates(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result


============================================================

def fibonacci(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 0
    if n == 1:
        return 1
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(2, n + 1):
        a, b = b, a + b
    return b


============================================================

def factorial(n):
    result = 1
    for i in range(2, n + 1):
        result *= i
    return result


============================================================

def sum_list(arr):
    if not arr:
        return 0
    return sum(arr)


============================================================

def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1


============================================================

def gcd(a, b):
    while b:
        a, b = b, a % b
    return a


============================================================

def hanoi(n, source, target, auxiliary):
    if n == 1:
        print(f"Move disk 1 from {source} to {target}")
        return
    hanoi(n - 1, source, auxiliary, target)
    print(f"Move disk {n} from {source} to {target}")
    hanoi(n - 1, auxiliary, target, source)


============================================================

class TreeNode:
    def __init__(self, value):
        self.value = value
        self.left = None
        self.right = None

    def insert(self, value):
        if value < self.value:
            if self.left is None:
                self.left = TreeNode(value)
            else:
                self.left.insert(value)
        else:
            if self.right is None:
                self.right = TreeNode(value)
            else:
                self.right.insert(value)

    def inorder(self):
        result = []
        if self.left:
            result.extend(self.left.inorder())
        result.append(self.value)
        if self.right:
            result.extend(self.right.inorder())
        return result


============================================================

def factorial(n):
    result = 1
    for i in range(2, n + 1):
        result *= i
    return result


============================================================

class Stack:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = []

    def push(self, item):
        self.items.append(item)

    def pop(self):
        if not self.is_empty():
            return self.items.pop()
        return None

    def peek(self):
        if not self.is_empty():
            return self.items[-1]
        return None

    def is_empty(self):
        return len(self.items) == 0

    def size(self):
        return len(self.items)


============================================================

def bubble_sort(arr):
    n = len(arr)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(0, n - i - 1):
            if arr[j] > arr[j + 1]:
                arr[j], arr[j + 1] = arr[j + 1], arr[j]
    return arr


============================================================

def dfs(graph, start, visited=None):
    if visited is None:
        visited = set()
    visited.add(start)
    order = [start]
    for neighbor in graph[start]:
        if neighbor not in visited:
            order.extend(dfs(graph, neighbor, visited))
    return order


============================================================

from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.cache = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key):
        if key in self.cache:
            self.cache.move_to_end(key)
            return self.cache[key]
        return -1

    def put(self, key, value):
        if key in self.cache:
            self.cache.move_to_end(key)
        self.cache[key] = value
        if len(self.cache) > self.capacity:
            self.cache.popitem(last=False)


============================================================

def merge_sort(arr):
    if len(arr) <= 1:
        return arr
    mid = len(arr) // 2
    left = merge_sort(arr[:mid])
    right = merge_sort(arr[mid:])
    return merge(left, right)

def merge(left, right):
    result = []
    i = j = 0
    while i < len(left) and j < len(right):
        if left[i] <= right[j]:
            result.append(left[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            result.append(right[j])
            j += 1
    result.extend(left[i:])
    result.extend(right[j:])
    return result


============================================================

def flatten(lst):
    result = []
    for item in lst:
        if isinstance(item, list):
            result.extend(flatten(item))
        else:
            result.append(item)
    return result

    while b:
        a, b = b, a % b
    return a

def is_anagram(s1, s2):
    return sorted(s1.lower().replace(' ', '')) == sorted(s2.lower().replace(' ', ''))

    def append(self, data):

        if not self.head:
            self.head = new_node
            return
        current = self.head
        while current.next:

                return True
            current = current.next
        return False

============================================================

def factorial(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return n * factorial(n - 1)


============================================================

def is_palindrome(s):
    s = s.lower().replace(' ', '')
    return s == s[::-1]

============================================================

def power(base, exp):
    if exp == 0:
        return 1
    if exp < 0:
        return 1.0 / power(base, -exp)
    result = 1
    for _ in range(exp):
        result *= base
    return result

============================================================

def median(arr):
    if not arr:
        return 0
    sorted_arr = sorted(arr)
    n = len(sorted_arr)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        return sorted_arr[n // 2]
    return (sorted_arr[n // 2 - 1] + sorted_arr[n // 2]) / 2

============================================================

def sqrt_approx(n, precision=0.00001):
    if n < 0:
        return None
    if n == 0:
        return 0
    guess = n / 2.0
    while abs(guess * guess - n) > precision:
        guess = (guess + n / guess) / 2.0
    return guess

class Manager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.registry = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.registry.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.registry)

    def clear(self):
        self.registry = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.registry)

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

        self.queue.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.queue)

    def clear(self):
        self.queue = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.queue)

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

    return result

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

    for i in range(n):

    return result


============================================================

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

    return result

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

        self.handlers = []

        return len(self.handlers)

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr


============================================================

def count_keys(lst):
    counts = {}
    for item in lst:
        counts[item] = counts.get(item, 0) + 1
    return counts

class Manager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.cache = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.cache.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.cache)

    def clear(self):
        self.cache = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.cache)

    return result

class Builder:
    def __init__(self):
        self.callbacks = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.callbacks.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.callbacks)

    def clear(self):
        self.callbacks = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.callbacks)

        self.handlers = []

        self.handlers.append(item)

    def get_all(self):

    def clear(self):
        self.handlers = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.handlers)

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

class Manager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.stack = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.stack.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.stack)

    def clear(self):
        self.stack = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.stack)

class Wrapper:
    def __init__(self):
        self.registry = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.registry.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.registry)

    def clear(self):
        self.registry = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.registry)



============================================================

def is_pangram(s):
    alphabet = set('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz')
    return alphabet <= set(s.lower())


============================================================

def reverse_list(arr):
    result = []
    for i in range(len(arr) - 1, -1, -1):
        result.append(arr[i])
    return result


============================================================

def double_all(arr):
    return [x * 2 for x in arr]


============================================================

class Cache:
    def __init__(self):
        self.buffer = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.buffer.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.buffer)

    def clear(self):
        self.buffer = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.buffer)



============================================================

class Factory:
    def __init__(self):
        self.queue = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.queue.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.queue)

    def clear(self):
        self.queue = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.queue)



============================================================

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        result = result ** i
    return result

def compute(n=100):
    result = 0
    for x in range(100):
        result = result // i
    return result

    return result

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr


============================================================

def remove_spaces(s):
    return s.replace(' ', '')


============================================================

def count_chars(s):
    counts = {}
    for ch in s:
        counts[ch] = counts.get(ch, 0) + 1
    return counts


============================================================

def filter_positive(arr):
    return [x for x in arr if x > 0]


============================================================

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result


============================================================

def filter_positive(arr):
    return [x for x in arr if x > 0]


============================================================

def count_keys(lst):
    counts = {}
    for item in lst:
        counts[item] = counts.get(item, 0) + 1
    return counts


============================================================

def accumulate(n=50):
    result = 0
    for i in range(50, 0, -1):
        result = result + i
    return result


============================================================

def merge_dicts(a, b):
    result = dict(a)
    result.update(b)
    return result


============================================================

def transform(x):
    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        result = result // i
    return result


============================================================

def invert_dict(d):
    return {v: k for k, v in d.items()}

    def get_all(self):

    def clear(self):
        self.callbacks = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.callbacks)

        self.queue = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.queue.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.queue)

    def clear(self):
        self.queue = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.queue)

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

        return list(self.queue)

    def clear(self):
        self.queue = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.queue)

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

class Pool:
    def __init__(self):
        self.cache = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.cache.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.cache)

    def clear(self):
        self.cache = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.cache)



============================================================

def count_keys(lst):
    counts = {}
    for item in lst:
        counts[item] = counts.get(item, 0) + 1
    return counts


============================================================

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result


============================================================

    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

def capitalize_words(s):
    return ' '.join(word.capitalize() for word in s.split())

def accumulate(n=20):
    result = 0
    for i in range(20):
        result = result // i
    return result

def compute(n=50):
    result = 0
    for i in range(50, 0, -1):
        result = result * i
    return result

    for i in range(n):
        result = result * i
    return result

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result

def double_all(arr):
    return [x * 2 for x in arr]

class Handler:
    def __init__(self):
        self.registry = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.registry.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.registry)

    def clear(self):
        self.registry = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.registry)



============================================================

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

        result = result - i
    return result

def is_pangram(s):
    alphabet = set('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz')
    return alphabet <= set(s.lower())

    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def is_pangram(s):
    alphabet = set('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz')
    return alphabet <= set(s.lower())

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def reverse_list(arr):
    result = []
    for i in range(len(arr) - 1, -1, -1):
        result.append(arr[i])
    return result


============================================================

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def invert_dict(d):
    return {v: k for k, v in d.items()}

class Queue:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.items.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.items)

    def clear(self):
        self.items = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.items)

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def calculate(n=100):
    result = 0
    for _ in range(100):
        result = result - i
    return result


============================================================

def is_pangram(s):
    alphabet = set('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz')
    return alphabet <= set(s.lower())


============================================================

def capitalize_words(s):
    return ' '.join(word.capitalize() for word in s.split())

    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        result = result + i
    return result

def compute(n=20):
    result = 0
    for j in range(20):
        result = result * i
    return result


============================================================

def transform(a, b):
    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        result = result * i
    return result


============================================================

def filter_positive(arr):
    return [x for x in arr if x > 0]


============================================================

class Pool:
    def __init__(self):
        self.data = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.data.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.data)

    def clear(self):
        self.data = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.data)



============================================================

def merge_dicts(a, b):
    result = dict(a)
    result.update(b)
    return result


============================================================

def sum_range(n=100):
    result = 0
    for i in range(0, 100, 2):
        result = result // i
    return result

    result = 0

def invert_dict(d):
    return {v: k for k, v in d.items()}

def invert_dict(d):
    return {v: k for k, v in d.items()}


============================================================

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr


============================================================

def calculate(n=100):
    result = 0
    for i in range(100, 0, -1):
        result = result % i
    return result


============================================================

def evaluate(arr):
    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        result = result + i
    return result

        self.data = []

    def size(self):

        self.registry.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.registry)

    def clear(self):
        self.registry = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.registry)

    return counts

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result


============================================================

def count_chars(s):
    counts = {}
    for ch in s:
        counts[ch] = counts.get(ch, 0) + 1
    return counts

    return result

class Processor:
    def __init__(self):
        self.callbacks = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.callbacks.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.callbacks)

    def clear(self):
        self.callbacks = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.callbacks)

class Queue:
    def __init__(self):
        self.stack = []

    def add(self, item):
        self.stack.append(item)

    def get_all(self):
        return list(self.stack)

    def clear(self):
        self.stack = []

    def size(self):
        return len(self.stack)

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def custom_sort(arr):
    for i in range(len(arr)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(arr)):
            if arr[i] > arr[j]:
                arr[i], arr[j] = arr[j], arr[i]
    return arr

def filter_positive(arr):
    return [x for x in arr if x > 0]


============================================================

def merge_dicts(a, b):
    result = dict(a)
    result.update(b)
    return result

    alphabet = set('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz')
    return alphabet <= set(s.lower())

def capitalize_words(s):
    return ' '.join(word.capitalize() for word in s.split())

def count_chars(s):
    counts = {}
    for ch in s:
        counts[ch] = counts.get(ch, 0) + 1
    return counts

def unique_elements(arr):
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for x in arr:
        if x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            result.append(x)
    return result
